The results demonstrate the usefulness of the presented method for downstream tasks, such as personalized therapy recommendations or toxicity warnings.

</details>


### [194] [On the Non-decoupling of Supervised Fine-tuning and Reinforcement Learning in Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07389)
*Xueyan Niu,Bo Bai,Wei Han,Weixi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SFT和RL在LLM后训练中不能解耦：先SFT后RL会增加SFT损失，先RL后SFT会降低奖励值


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推理模型广泛采用交替进行SFT和RL训练的做法，但缺乏理论分析这两种方法是否可以解耦。本文旨在从理论上证明SFT和RL在LLM后训练中不能分离而不损失先前性能。

Method: 通过理论证明两个耦合关系：(1) SFT-then-RL耦合：在SFT最优性条件下，RL会增加SFT损失；(2) RL-then-SFT耦合：SFT会降低RL实现的奖励值。实验使用Qwen3-0.6B模型验证理论预测的性能下降。

Result: 理论证明SFT和RL不能解耦：先SFT后RL会增加SFT损失，先RL后SFT会降低奖励值。Qwen3-0.6B实验证实了预测的性能下降，验证了SFT和RL分离会导致先前性能损失。

Conclusion: SFT和RL在LLM后训练中具有不可解耦的耦合关系，交替训练是必要的。分离这两种训练方法会导致性能下降，这解释了为什么现代推理模型需要交替进行SFT和RL训练。

Abstract: Post-training of large language models routinely interleaves supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with reinforcement learning (RL). These two methods have different objectives: SFT minimizes the cross-entropy loss between model outputs and expert responses, while RL maximizes reward signals derived from human preferences or rule-based verifiers. Modern reasoning models have widely adopted the practice of alternating SFT and RL training. However, there is no theoretical account of whether they can be decoupled. We prove that decoupling is impossible in either order: (1) SFT-then-RL coupling: RL increases SFT loss under SFT optimality and (2) RL-then-SFT coupling: SFT lowers the reward achieved by RL. Experiments on Qwen3-0.6B confirm the predicted degradation, verifying that SFT and RL cannot be separated without loss of prior performance in the post-training

</details>


### [195] [OceanSAR-2: A Universal Feature Extractor for SAR Ocean Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07392)
*Alexandre Tuel,Thomas Kerdreux,Quentin Febvre,Alexis Mouche,Antoine Grouazel,Jean-Renaud Miadana,Antoine Audras,Chen Wang,Bertrand Chapron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OceanSAR-2是第二代SAR海洋观测基础模型，通过改进的自监督学习和动态数据管理策略提升性能并降低训练成本，在多种下游任务中表现出色，并发布了标准化基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在第一代模型基础上，开发更高效、性能更强的SAR海洋观测基础模型，解决现有模型训练成本高、泛化能力有限的问题，为SAR海洋应用提供系统化的评估基准。

Method: 采用改进的自监督学习（SSL）训练方法和动态数据管理策略，基于Sentinel-1 Wave Mode数据，通过优化训练流程降低计算成本，同时提升模型性能。

Result: OceanSAR-2在多个下游任务中表现出强大的迁移性能，包括地球物理模式分类、海面风矢量估计、有效波高估算和冰山检测，同时训练成本显著降低。

Conclusion: OceanSAR-2作为第二代SAR海洋观测基础模型，通过技术改进实现了性能提升和成本优化，发布的标准化基准数据集将为SAR海洋应用模型的系统评估和发展奠定基础。

Abstract: We present OceanSAR-2, the second generation of our foundation model for SAR-based ocean observation. Building on our earlier release, which pioneered self-supervised learning on Sentinel-1 Wave Mode data, OceanSAR-2 relies on improved SSL training and dynamic data curation strategies, which enhances performance while reducing training cost. OceanSAR-2 demonstrates strong transfer performance across downstream tasks, including geophysical pattern classification, ocean surface wind vector and significant wave height estimation, and iceberg detection. We release standardized benchmark datasets, providing a foundation for systematic evaluation and advancement of SAR models for ocean applications.

</details>


### [196] [The Practicality of Normalizing Flow Test-Time Training in Bayesian Inference for Agent-Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07413)
*Junyao Zhang,Jinglai Li,Junqi Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次将测试时训练应用于基于智能体的模型参数后验估计，提出多种测试时训练策略来微调归一化流以应对分布偏移


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于智能体的模型在经济学和社会科学中越来越受欢迎，因为它们能够灵活描述个体智能体之间的现实异质性决策和交互规则。然而，在ABM参数后验估计中，深度模型（如归一化流）面临分布偏移的挑战，需要实时调整能力。

Method: 首次将测试时训练应用于ABM参数后验估计，提出多种实用的TTT策略来微调归一化流以应对分布偏移。这些策略旨在使基于流的推理能够实时适应ABM参数的变化。

Result: 数值研究表明，测试时训练方案非常有效，能够实现基于流的ABM参数推理的实时调整。提出的TTT策略显著提升了归一化流在分布偏移情况下的适应性。

Conclusion: 测试时训练为基于智能体的模型参数后验估计提供了一种实用的解决方案，能够有效应对分布偏移问题，实现实时参数推理调整，增强了深度模型在ABM应用中的实用性。

Abstract: Agent-Based Models (ABMs) are gaining great popularity in economics and social science because of their strong flexibility to describe the realistic and heterogeneous decisions and interaction rules between individual agents. In this work, we investigate for the first time the practicality of test-time training (TTT) of deep models such as normalizing flows, in the parameters posterior estimations of ABMs. We propose several practical TTT strategies for fine-tuning the normalizing flow against distribution shifts. Our numerical study demonstrates that TTT schemes are remarkably effective, enabling real-time adjustment of flow-based inference for ABM parameters.

</details>


### [197] [Variational Autoencoder with Normalizing flow for X-ray spectral fitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07440)
*Fiona Redmen,Ethan Tregidga,James F. Steiner,Cecilia Garraffo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用变分自编码器与归一化流构建概率模型，显著提升黑洞X射线双星光谱拟合效率，比传统MCMC方法快三个数量级


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的光谱拟合方法（如马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛MCMC）在计算时间上存在限制，无法满足黑洞X射线双星研究中快速获取物理参数的需求

Method: 提出一种概率模型，结合变分自编码器和归一化流，训练模型采用物理潜在空间，能够预测光谱模型参数及其完整概率分布

Result: 相比之前的确定性模型，光谱重建质量显著提升；计算速度比传统方法快三个数量级（1000倍）

Conclusion: 该概率模型为黑洞X射线双星研究提供了一种高效、准确的光谱拟合方法，能够在极端引力环境中快速获取物理约束

Abstract: Black hole X-ray binaries (BHBs) can be studied with spectral fitting to provide physical constraints on accretion in extreme gravitational environments. Traditional methods of spectral fitting such as Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) face limitations due to computational times. We introduce a probabilistic model, utilizing a variational autoencoder with a normalizing flow, trained to adopt a physical latent space. This neural network produces predictions for spectral-model parameters as well as their full probability distributions. Our implementations result in a significant improvement in spectral reconstructions over a previous deterministic model while performing three orders of magnitude faster than traditional methods.

</details>


### [198] [Task Prototype-Based Knowledge Retrieval for Multi-Task Learning from Partially Annotated Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07474)
*Youngmin Oh,Hyung-Il Kim,Jung Uk Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于原型知识检索的框架，解决部分标注多任务学习中的负迁移问题，通过任务原型捕获任务特性并量化任务关联，避免依赖未标注任务的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中多任务学习需要同时处理多个任务，但获取所有任务的完整标注数据成本高昂。现有部分标注多任务学习方法依赖未标注任务的预测，难以建立可靠的任务关联，可能导致负迁移和性能下降。

Method: 提出原型知识检索框架：1）任务原型嵌入任务特定特征并量化任务关联；2）知识检索transformer基于这些关联自适应优化特征表示。引入关联知识生成损失确保任务原型持续捕获任务特定特征。

Result: 大量实验证明该框架的有效性，即使在只有部分任务被标注的情况下，也能实现鲁棒的多任务学习。

Conclusion: 该原型知识检索框架为部分标注多任务学习提供了有效解决方案，通过避免依赖未标注任务的预测，建立了可靠的任务关联，减少了负迁移风险，提升了学习性能。

Abstract: Multi-task learning (MTL) is critical in real-world applications such as autonomous driving and robotics, enabling simultaneous handling of diverse tasks. However, obtaining fully annotated data for all tasks is impractical due to labeling costs. Existing methods for partially labeled MTL typically rely on predictions from unlabeled tasks, making it difficult to establish reliable task associations and potentially leading to negative transfer and suboptimal performance. To address these issues, we propose a prototype-based knowledge retrieval framework that achieves robust MTL instead of relying on predictions from unlabeled tasks. Our framework consists of two key components: (1) a task prototype embedding task-specific characteristics and quantifying task associations, and (2) a knowledge retrieval transformer that adaptively refines feature representations based on these associations. To achieve this, we introduce an association knowledge generating (AKG) loss to ensure the task prototype consistently captures task-specific characteristics. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework, highlighting its potential for robust multi-task learning, even when only a subset of tasks is annotated.

</details>


### [199] [ARCQuant: Boosting NVFP4 Quantization with Augmented Residual Channels for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07475)
*Haoqian Meng,Yilun Luo,Yafei Zhao,Wenyuan Liu,Peng Zhang,Xindian Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARCQuant框架通过增强残差通道提升NVFP4格式性能，在保持统一NVFP4格式的同时实现接近全精度基线的精度，并在GPU上获得3倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度数值格式如NVFP4为LLM推理提供了新机会，但现有PTQ策略难以适配：旋转方法破坏块隔离，平滑技术难以处理4位量化误差，混合精度方法违反硬件统一精度计算约束

Method: 提出ARCQuant框架，通过增强残差通道保持统一NVFP4格式，在激活矩阵中增加量化残差通道，将误差补偿过程直接集成到矩阵约简维度，可使用标准优化GEMM核函数

Result: 理论分析显示双阶段NVFP4量化的最坏情况误差界与MXFP8等标准8位格式相当；LLaMA和Qwen模型实验显示在困惑度和下游任务上达到SOTA精度，接近全精度基线；RTX 5090和RTX PRO 6000 GPU部署实现最高3倍于FP16的加速

Conclusion: ARCQuant成功解决了NVFP4格式的PTQ适配问题，在保持硬件友好统一格式的同时实现了高精度和高效推理，为细粒度数值格式在LLM中的应用提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: The emergence of fine-grained numerical formats like NVFP4 presents new opportunities for efficient Large Language Model (LLM) inference. However, it is difficult to adapt existing Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) strategies to these formats: rotation-based methods compromise fine-grained block isolation; smoothing techniques struggle with significant 4-bit quantization errors; and mixed-precision approaches often conflict with hardware constraints on unified-precision computation. To address these challenges, we propose ARCQuant, a framework that boosts NVFP4 performance via Augmented Residual Channels. Distinct from methods that compromise block isolation or hardware uniformity, ARCQuant maintains a strictly unified NVFP4 format by augmenting the activation matrix with quantized residual channels. This design integrates the error compensation process directly into the matrix reduction dimension, enabling the use of standard, highly optimized GEMM kernels with minimal overhead. Theoretical analysis confirms that the worst-case error bound of our dual-stage NVFP4 quantization is comparable to that of standard 8-bit formats such as MXFP8. Extensive experiments on LLaMA and Qwen models demonstrate that ARCQuant achieves state-of-the-art accuracy, comparable to full-precision baselines in perplexity and downstream tasks. Furthermore, deployment on RTX 5090 and RTX PRO 6000 GPUs confirms practical benefits, achieving up to 3x speedup over FP16. Our code is available at https://github.com/actypedef/ARCQuant .

</details>


### [200] [Graph Inference Towards ICD Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07496)
*Xiaoxiao Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LabGraph将ICD编码重新定义为图生成任务，通过对抗域适应、图强化学习和扰动正则化提升模型鲁棒性，在基准数据集上表现优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动ICD编码面临巨大标签空间和极端类别不平衡的挑战，现有方法难以实现精确预测，需要新的框架来提升模型鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出LabGraph统一框架，将ICD编码重新定义为图生成任务，结合对抗域适应、图强化学习和扰动正则化，并引入标签图判别器动态评估生成代码，提供自适应奖励反馈。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，LabGraph在micro-F1、micro-AUC和P@K指标上持续优于先前方法。

Conclusion: LabGraph通过图生成框架有效解决了ICD编码中的标签空间大和类别不平衡问题，提升了模型的鲁棒性和泛化性能。

Abstract: Automated ICD coding involves assigning standardized diagnostic codes to clinical narratives. The vast label space and extreme class imbalance continue to challenge precise prediction. To address these issues, LabGraph is introduced -- a unified framework that reformulates ICD coding as a graph generation task. By combining adversarial domain adaptation, graph-based reinforcement learning, and perturbation regularization, LabGraph effectively enhances model robustness and generalization. In addition, a label graph discriminator dynamically evaluates each generated code, providing adaptive reward feedback during training. Experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that LabGraph consistently outperforms previous approaches on micro-F1, micro-AUC, and P@K.

</details>


### [201] [FROAV: A Framework for RAG Observation and Agent Verification - Lowering the Barrier to LLM Agent Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07504)
*Tzu-Hsuan Lin,Chih-Hsuan Kao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FROAV是一个开源研究平台，通过可视化工作流编排、综合评估框架和可扩展Python集成，降低LLM智能体研究的门槛，使研究人员无需编写基础设施代码即可快速原型化RAG策略和验证智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM及其在自主智能体系统中的集成虽然为文档分析、决策支持和知识检索创造了前所未有的机会，但开发、评估和迭代基于LLM的智能体工作流的复杂性给研究人员（尤其是缺乏广泛软件工程专业知识的研究人员）带来了重大障碍。

Method: FROAV采用多阶段检索增强生成（RAG）管道与严格的"LLM-as-a-Judge"评估系统相结合的方法。该框架集成了n8n用于无代码工作流设计、PostgreSQL用于细粒度数据管理、FastAPI用于灵活的后端逻辑、Streamlit用于人在环交互，通过直观的图形界面提供可插拔架构。

Result: 通过金融文档分析的应用展示了该框架的实用性，同时强调了其材料无关的架构，能够适应任何需要语义分析的领域。该平台使研究人员能够快速原型化RAG策略、进行提示工程实验、根据人类判断验证智能体性能并收集结构化反馈。

Conclusion: FROAV代表了向更广泛科学社区开放LLM智能体研究的重要一步，使研究人员能够专注于假设测试和算法创新，而不是系统集成挑战，从而民主化了LLM智能体研究。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) and their integration into autonomous agent systems has created unprecedented opportunities for document analysis, decision support, and knowledge retrieval. However, the complexity of developing, evaluating, and iterating on LLM-based agent workflows presents significant barriers to researchers, particularly those without extensive software engineering expertise. We present FROAV (Framework for RAG Observation and Agent Verification), an open-source research platform that democratizes LLM agent research by providing a plug-and-play architecture combining visual workflow orchestration, a comprehensive evaluation framework, and extensible Python integration. FROAV implements a multi-stage Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline coupled with a rigorous "LLM-as-a-Judge" evaluation system, all accessible through intuitive graphical interfaces. Our framework integrates n8n for no-code workflow design, PostgreSQL for granular data management, FastAPI for flexible backend logic, and Streamlit for human-in-the-loop interaction. Through this integrated ecosystem, researchers can rapidly prototype RAG strategies, conduct prompt engineering experiments, validate agent performance against human judgments, and collect structured feedback-all without writing infrastructure code. We demonstrate the framework's utility through its application to financial document analysis, while emphasizing its material-agnostic architecture that adapts to any domain requiring semantic analysis. FROAV represents a significant step toward making LLM agent research accessible to a broader scientific community, enabling researchers to focus on hypothesis testing and algorithmic innovation rather than system integration challenges.

</details>


### [202] [Stagewise Reinforcement Learning and the Geometry of the Regret Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07524)
*Chris Elliott,Einar Urdshals,David Quarel,Matthew Farrugia-Roberts,Daniel Murfet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将奇异学习理论扩展到深度强化学习，证明策略后验集中由局部学习系数(LLC)控制，预测贝叶斯相变从高遗憾简单策略向低遗憾复杂策略演化，在网格世界环境中验证了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将奇异学习理论从监督学习扩展到强化学习领域，研究贝叶斯学习在强化学习中的相变现象，理解策略演化过程中准确性与复杂性之间的权衡。

Method: 理论证明策略后验集中由局部学习系数(LLC)控制，LLC是遗憾函数几何结构的不变量。在网格世界环境中进行实证验证，通过SGD训练观察相变现象，分析遗憾和LLC的变化模式。

Result: 理论预测得到实证验证：相变表现为"对立阶梯"模式，遗憾急剧下降时LLC增加。LLC能检测到在部分状态上策略看似相同（遗憾相同）时的相变，表明它捕捉的是底层算法的变化而非仅性能变化。

Conclusion: 奇异学习理论成功扩展到深度强化学习，局部学习系数(LLC)作为几何不变量能有效表征贝叶斯相变，揭示了策略演化从简单高遗憾到复杂低遗憾的阶段性发展过程。

Abstract: Singular learning theory characterizes Bayesian learning as an evolving tradeoff between accuracy and complexity, with transitions between qualitatively different solutions as sample size increases. We extend this theory to deep reinforcement learning, proving that the concentration of the generalized posterior over policies is governed by the local learning coefficient (LLC), an invariant of the geometry of the regret function. This theory predicts that Bayesian phase transitions in reinforcement learning should proceed from simple policies with high regret to complex policies with low regret. We verify this prediction empirically in a gridworld environment exhibiting stagewise policy development: phase transitions over SGD training manifest as "opposing staircases" where regret decreases sharply while the LLC increases. Notably, the LLC detects phase transitions even when estimated on a subset of states where the policies appear identical in terms of regret, suggesting it captures changes in the underlying algorithm rather than just performance.

</details>


### [203] [Near-Optimal Private Linear Regression via Iterative Hessian Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07545)
*Omri Lev,Moshe Shenfeld,Vishwak Srinivasan,Katrina Ligett,Ashia C. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高斯草图的新型差分隐私普通最小二乘法（DP-OLS）算法——迭代Hessian混合，该算法在多个基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然自适应充分统计扰动（AdaSSP）方法在DP-OLS中表现优异，但基于高斯草图的方法在联邦和分布式回归中广泛应用，却在DP-OLS中相对少见。本文旨在探索高斯草图方法在DP-OLS中的潜力，以克服现有方法的固有局限性。

Method: 提出迭代Hessian混合算法，这是一种基于高斯草图的DP-OLS方法，灵感来源于迭代Hessian草图算法。该方法通过预乘随机高斯矩阵来确保差分隐私，并对先前的高斯草图方法进行了新的理论分析。

Result: 迭代Hessian混合算法克服了先前方法的固有局限性，相比AdaSSP提供了非平凡的改进。在标准基准测试中的广泛实验表明，该方法始终优于先前的基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于高斯草图的迭代Hessian混合算法为DP-OLS提供了一种有效的新方法，在理论和实证上都表现出优越性能，为差分隐私线性回归开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: We study differentially private ordinary least squares (DP-OLS) with bounded data. The dominant approach, adaptive sufficient-statistics perturbation (AdaSSP), adds an adaptively chosen perturbation to the sufficient statistics, namely, the matrix $X^{\top}X$ and the vector $X^{\top}Y$, and is known to achieve near-optimal accuracy and to have strong empirical performance. In contrast, methods that rely on Gaussian-sketching, which ensure differential privacy by pre-multiplying the data with a random Gaussian matrix, are widely used in federated and distributed regression, yet remain relatively uncommon for DP-OLS. In this work, we introduce the iterative Hessian mixing, a novel DP-OLS algorithm that relies on Gaussian sketches and is inspired by the iterative Hessian sketch algorithm. We provide utility analysis for the iterative Hessian mixing as well as a new analysis for the previous methods that rely on Gaussian sketches. Then, we show that our new approach circumvents the intrinsic limitations of the prior methods and provides non-trivial improvements over AdaSSP. We conclude by running an extensive set of experiments across standard benchmarks to demonstrate further that our approach consistently outperforms these prior baselines.

</details>


### [204] [Contextual Discrepancy-Aware Contrastive Learning for Robust Medical Time Series Diagnosis in Small-Sample Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07548)
*Kaito Tanaka,Aya Nakayama,Masato Ito,Yuji Nishimura,Keisuke Matsuda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoDAC是一种针对医疗时间序列数据的上下文差异感知对比学习框架，通过利用外部健康数据和动态多视图对比机制，有效解决数据稀缺问题并提升小样本场景下的诊断准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗时间序列数据（如EEG、ECG）对神经和心血管疾病诊断至关重要，但面临高标注成本导致的数据稀缺问题，且传统对比学习方法难以捕捉复杂的时间模式。

Method: 提出CoDAC框架：1）利用外部健康数据；2）引入基于Transformer自编码器的上下文差异估计器（CDE），通过上下文感知异常评分量化异常信号；3）动态多视图对比框架（DMCF）根据差异评分自适应加权不同时间视图，聚焦诊断相关区域；4）编码器结合扩张卷积和多头注意力进行特征提取。

Result: 在阿尔茨海默病EEG、帕金森病EEG和心肌梗死ECG数据集上的实验表明，CoDAC在所有指标上均优于现有基线方法，尤其在低标签可用性条件下表现突出。消融研究验证了CDE和DMCF的关键贡献。

Conclusion: CoDAC为医疗时间序列诊断提供了鲁棒且可解释的解决方案，有效缓解了数据稀缺挑战，在临床应用中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Medical time series data, such as EEG and ECG, are vital for diagnosing neurological and cardiovascular diseases. However, their precise interpretation faces significant challenges due to high annotation costs, leading to data scarcity, and the limitations of traditional contrastive learning in capturing complex temporal patterns. To address these issues, we propose CoDAC (Contextual Discrepancy-Aware Contrastive learning), a novel framework that enhances diagnostic accuracy and generalization, particularly in small-sample settings. CoDAC leverages external healthy data and introduces a Contextual Discrepancy Estimator (CDE), built upon a Transformer-based Autoencoder, to precisely quantify abnormal signals through context-aware anomaly scores. These scores dynamically inform a Dynamic Multi-views Contrastive Framework (DMCF), which adaptively weights different temporal views to focus contrastive learning on diagnostically relevant, discrepant regions. Our encoder combines dilated convolutions with multi-head attention for robust feature extraction. Comprehensive experiments on Alzheimer's Disease EEG, Parkinson's Disease EEG, and Myocardial Infarction ECG datasets demonstrate CoDAC's superior performance across all metrics, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art baselines, especially under low label availability. Ablation studies further validate the critical contributions of CDE and DMCF. CoDAC offers a robust and interpretable solution for medical time series diagnosis, effectively mitigating data scarcity challenges.

</details>


### [205] [TFEC: Multivariate Time-Series Clustering via Temporal-Frequency Enhanced Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07550)
*Zexi Tan,Tao Xie,Haoyi Xiao,Baoyao Yang,Yuzhu Ji,An Zeng,Xiang Zhang,Yiqun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TFEC框架通过时频协同增强机制和双路径学习，解决了现有对比学习模型在多元时间序列聚类中忽略聚类信息和引入不合理归纳偏置的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于对比学习的多元时间序列聚类模型存在两个关键局限：1) 在正负样本对构建时忽略聚类信息；2) 通过增强策略破坏时间依赖性和周期性等不合理归纳偏置，损害表示质量。

Method: 提出时频增强对比学习框架，包含时频协同增强机制以保持时间结构并生成低失真表示，设计协同双路径表示和聚类分布学习框架，联合优化聚类结构和表示保真度。

Result: 在六个真实世界基准数据集上的实验表明，TFEC优于现有最先进方法，平均NMI提升4.48%，消融研究验证了设计有效性。

Conclusion: TFEC框架通过时频协同增强和双路径学习，有效解决了现有对比学习模型在多元时间序列聚类中的局限性，显著提升了聚类性能。

Abstract: Multivariate Time-Series (MTS) clustering is crucial for signal processing and data analysis. Although deep learning approaches, particularly those leveraging Contrastive Learning (CL), are prominent for MTS representation, existing CL-based models face two key limitations: 1) neglecting clustering information during positive/negative sample pair construction, and 2) introducing unreasonable inductive biases, e.g., destroying time dependence and periodicity through augmentation strategies, compromising representation quality. This paper, therefore, proposes a Temporal-Frequency Enhanced Contrastive (TFEC) learning framework. To preserve temporal structure while generating low-distortion representations, a temporal-frequency Co-EnHancement (CoEH) mechanism is introduced. Accordingly, a synergistic dual-path representation and cluster distribution learning framework is designed to jointly optimize cluster structure and representation fidelity. Experiments on six real-world benchmark datasets demonstrate TFEC's superiority, achieving 4.48% average NMI gains over SOTA methods, with ablation studies validating the design. The code of the paper is available at: https://github.com/yueliangy/TFEC.

</details>


### [206] [d3LLM: Ultra-Fast Diffusion LLM using Pseudo-Trajectory Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07568)
*Yu-Yang Qian,Junda Su,Lanxiang Hu,Peiyuan Zhang,Zhijie Deng,Peng Zhao,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: d3LLM通过伪轨迹蒸馏和基于熵的多块解码技术，在保持准确性的同时实现了扩散大语言模型的高并行性，相比现有方法获得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型(dLLMs)相比自回归模型具有并行解码和随机顺序生成的优势，但实践中面临准确性与并行性的权衡。现有方法通常只关注效率或性能的单一维度，需要一种平衡两者性能的解决方案。

Method: 提出d3LLM框架：训练阶段采用伪轨迹蒸馏技术，教导模型哪些token可以在早期步骤中自信地解码；推理阶段使用基于熵的多块解码配合KV缓存刷新机制，在保持准确性的同时实现高并行性。

Result: 实验表明d3LLM相比vanilla LLaDA/Dream实现高达10倍加速，相比自回归模型实现5倍加速，且准确性下降很小。同时提出了AUP（准确性下的并行性）这一联合评估指标。

Conclusion: d3LLM通过创新的训练和推理策略，成功解决了扩散大语言模型的准确性与并行性权衡问题，为高效文本生成提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer capabilities beyond those of autoregressive (AR) LLMs, such as parallel decoding and random-order generation. However, realizing these benefits in practice is non-trivial, as dLLMs inherently face an accuracy-parallelism trade-off. Despite increasing interest, existing methods typically focus on only one-side of the coin, targeting either efficiency or performance. To address this limitation, we propose d3LLM (Pseudo-Distilled Diffusion Large Language Model), striking a balance between accuracy and parallelism: (i) during training, we introduce pseudo-trajectory distillation to teach the model which tokens can be decoded confidently at early steps, thereby improving parallelism; (ii) during inference, we employ entropy-based multi-block decoding with a KV-cache refresh mechanism to achieve high parallelism while maintaining accuracy. To better evaluate dLLMs, we also introduce AUP (Accuracy Under Parallelism), a new metric that jointly measures accuracy and parallelism. Experiments demonstrate that our d3LLM achieves up to 10$\times$ speedup over vanilla LLaDA/Dream and 5$\times$ speedup over AR models without much accuracy drop. Our code is available at https://github.com/hao-ai-lab/d3LLM.

</details>


### [207] [Neural Architecture for Fast and Reliable Coagulation Assessment in Clinical Settings: Leveraging Thromboelastography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07618)
*Yulu Wang,Ziqian Zeng,Jianjun Wu,Zhifeng Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PSR算法利用小样本临床数据，通过多域特征提取和注意力机制，实现凝血状态的实时监测，显著提升预测精度并减少推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统血栓弹力图需要近1小时才能提供结果，这种延迟可能导致死亡率上升。医学AI面临的关键挑战是如何基于小数据集进行合理预测，并考虑不同患者群体间的变异，而传统深度学习方法在这方面表现不佳。

Method: 提出生理状态重建算法，包括：MDFE用于整合多时间域信号的多域学习；HLA联合学习高层时间交互和注意力机制；参数化DAM维持计算的生命体征稳定性。

Result: 在4个TEG专用数据集上评估，凝血特征预测R2>0.98，误差比最先进方法减少约一半，推理时间也减半。

Conclusion: 漂移感知学习为医学AI开辟了新方向，特别是在数据稀缺的医疗应用中，其潜力不仅限于血栓发现，还可扩展到其他医学AI应用。

Abstract: In an ideal medical environment, real-time coagulation monitoring can enable early detection and prompt remediation of risks. However, traditional Thromboelastography (TEG), a widely employed diagnostic modality, can only provide such outputs after nearly 1 hour of measurement. The delay might lead to elevated mortality rates. These issues clearly point out one of the key challenges for medical AI development: Mak-ing reasonable predictions based on very small data sets and accounting for variation between different patient populations, a task where conventional deep learning methods typically perform poorly. We present Physiological State Reconstruc-tion (PSR), a new algorithm specifically designed to take ad-vantage of dynamic changes between individuals and to max-imize useful information produced by small amounts of clini-cal data through mapping to reliable predictions and diagnosis. We develop MDFE to facilitate integration of varied temporal signals using multi-domain learning, and jointly learn high-level temporal interactions together with attentions via HLA; furthermore, the parameterized DAM we designed maintains the stability of the computed vital signs. PSR evaluates with 4 TEG-specialized data sets and establishes remarkable perfor-mance -- predictions of R2 > 0.98 for coagulation traits and error reduction around half compared to the state-of-the-art methods, and halving the inferencing time too. Drift-aware learning suggests a new future, with potential uses well be-yond thrombophilia discovery towards medical AI applica-tions with data scarcity.

</details>


### [208] [Beyond Sharpness: A Flatness Decomposition Framework for Efficient Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07636)
*Yanan Chen,Tieliang Gong,Yunjiao Zhang,Wen Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLAD：一种用于持续学习的轻量级优化框架，通过分解锐度感知扰动为梯度对齐和随机噪声分量，仅保留噪声分量来提升泛化能力，同时减少计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习中的锐度感知方法存在两个关键限制：1) 将锐度正则化视为统一信号而未区分其组件的贡献；2) 引入大量计算开销阻碍实际部署。

Method: 提出FLAD优化框架，将锐度感知扰动分解为梯度对齐和随机噪声分量，仅保留噪声分量以促进泛化。引入轻量级调度方案，在有限训练时间内保持性能增益。可无缝集成到各种持续学习范式中。

Result: FLAD在多种实验设置中一致优于标准和锐度感知优化器，展示了其在持续学习中的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: FLAD通过分解锐度感知扰动并仅保留噪声分量，实现了更好的泛化能力和更低的计算开销，为持续学习提供了一种实用且有效的优化框架。

Abstract: Continual Learning (CL) aims to enable models to sequentially learn multiple tasks without forgetting previous knowledge. Recent studies have shown that optimizing towards flatter loss minima can improve model generalization. However, existing sharpness-aware methods for CL suffer from two key limitations: (1) they treat sharpness regularization as a unified signal without distinguishing the contributions of its components. and (2) they introduce substantial computational overhead that impedes practical deployment. To address these challenges, we propose FLAD, a novel optimization framework that decomposes sharpness-aware perturbations into gradient-aligned and stochastic-noise components, and show that retaining only the noise component promotes generalization. We further introduce a lightweight scheduling scheme that enables FLAD to maintain significant performance gains even under constrained training time. FLAD can be seamlessly integrated into various CL paradigms and consistently outperforms standard and sharpness-aware optimizers in diverse experimental settings, demonstrating its effectiveness and practicality in CL.

</details>


### [209] [Improving Domain Generalization in Contrastive Learning using Adaptive Temperature Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07748)
*Robert Lewis,Katie Matton,Rosalind W. Picard,John Guttag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的对比学习方法，通过调整InfoNCE损失中的温度参数来增强学习表示的领域不变性，从而改善分布外泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当训练数据来自多个领域而测试数据来自未见领域且存在显著协变量偏移时，传统的自监督对比学习方法性能会大幅下降。需要开发能够增强表示领域不变性的方法以改善分布外泛化。

Method: 提出一种新的对比学习方法，通过利用领域标签调整InfoNCE损失中的温度参数。具体而言，根据负样本与锚点来自同一领域的概率来调整温度，从而对来自更相似领域的负样本对赋予更高权重，鼓励模型基于领域不变属性进行样本区分。

Result: 在MNIST变体数据集上的实验表明，该方法在分布外性能上优于领域泛化基线方法。同时，该方法保持了强大的分布内任务性能，在这一指标上显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过调整对比学习中的温度参数来增强表示学习的领域不变性，能够有效改善模型在未见领域上的泛化能力，同时保持原有的分布内性能，为处理协变量偏移问题提供了一种有效解决方案。

Abstract: Self-supervised pre-training with contrastive learning is a powerful method for learning from sparsely labeled data. However, performance can drop considerably when there is a shift in the distribution of data from training to test time. We study this phenomenon in a setting in which the training data come from multiple domains, and the test data come from a domain not seen at training that is subject to significant covariate shift. We present a new method for contrastive learning that incorporates domain labels to increase the domain invariance of learned representations, leading to improved out-of-distribution generalization. Our method adjusts the temperature parameter in the InfoNCE loss -- which controls the relative weighting of negative pairs -- using the probability that a negative sample comes from the same domain as the anchor. This upweights pairs from more similar domains, encouraging the model to discriminate samples based on domain-invariant attributes. Through experiments on a variant of the MNIST dataset, we demonstrate that our method yields better out-of-distribution performance than domain generalization baselines. Furthermore, our method maintains strong in-distribution task performance, substantially outperforming baselines on this measure.

</details>


### [210] [Are LLM Decisions Faithful to Verbal Confidence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07767)
*Jiawei Wang,Yanfei Zhou,Siddartha Devic,Deqing Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLMs虽然能表达不确定性，但缺乏根据风险调整决策的战略能力，导致在高压惩罚条件下仍不选择弃权，造成效用崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLMs表达的不确定性是否与其推理、知识或决策能力相关，以及模型是否具备根据风险调整决策的战略能力。

Method: 引入RiskEval框架，评估模型在不同错误惩罚条件下是否调整其弃权策略，测试多个前沿模型在高压惩罚条件下的决策行为。

Result: 模型在表达口头置信度时缺乏成本意识，在高惩罚条件下缺乏战略响应能力；即使频繁弃权是最优数学策略，模型也几乎从不弃权，导致效用崩溃。

Conclusion: 校准的口头置信度分数不足以创建可信赖和可解释的AI系统，当前模型缺乏将不确定性信号转化为最优风险敏感决策的战略能动性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can produce surprisingly sophisticated estimates of their own uncertainty. However, it remains unclear to what extent this expressed confidence is tied to the reasoning, knowledge, or decision making of the model. To test this, we introduce $\textbf{RiskEval}$: a framework designed to evaluate whether models adjust their abstention policies in response to varying error penalties. Our evaluation of several frontier models reveals a critical dissociation: models are neither cost-aware when articulating their verbal confidence, nor strategically responsive when deciding whether to engage or abstain under high-penalty conditions. Even when extreme penalties render frequent abstention the mathematically optimal strategy, models almost never abstain, resulting in utility collapse. This indicates that calibrated verbal confidence scores may not be sufficient to create trustworthy and interpretable AI systems, as current models lack the strategic agency to convert uncertainty signals into optimal and risk-sensitive decisions.

</details>


### [211] [DT-ICU: Towards Explainable Digital Twins for ICU Patient Monitoring via Multi-Modal and Multi-Task Iterative Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07778)
*Wen Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DT-ICU是一个用于重症监护连续风险估计的多模态数字孪生框架，整合临床时间序列和静态信息，在MIMIC-IV数据集上优于基线模型，提供准确、稳健且可解释的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个能够整合多模态临床数据、支持连续风险估计的数字孪生框架，以改善重症监护中的患者监测和预测能力。

Method: 提出DT-ICU框架，整合可变长度临床时间序列和静态患者信息，采用统一的多任务架构，支持随着ICU住院期间新观察数据的积累而更新预测。

Result: 在大型公开数据集MIMIC-IV上评估，DT-ICU在不同评估设置下始终优于基线模型；测试长度分析显示入院后短期内即可实现有意义的区分，更长观察窗口可进一步提高高风险患者的排名；模态消融实验表明模型合理利用了干预措施、生理反应观察和上下文信息。

Conclusion: DT-ICU提供了准确、时间稳健且可解释的预测，展示了其作为重症监护连续患者监测实用数字孪生框架的潜力。源代码和训练模型权重已公开。

Abstract: We introduce DT-ICU, a multimodal digital twin framework for continuous risk estimation in intensive care. DT-ICU integrates variable-length clinical time series with static patient information in a unified multitask architecture, enabling predictions to be updated as new observations accumulate over the ICU stay. We evaluate DT-ICU on the large, publicly available MIMIC-IV dataset, where it consistently outperforms established baseline models under different evaluation settings. Our test-length analysis shows that meaningful discrimination is achieved shortly after admission, while longer observation windows further improve the ranking of high-risk patients in highly imbalanced cohorts. To examine how the model leverages heterogeneous data sources, we perform systematic modality ablations, revealing that the model learnt a reasonable structured reliance on interventions, physiological response observations, and contextual information. These analyses provide interpretable insights into how multimodal signals are combined and how trade-offs between sensitivity and precision emerge. Together, these results demonstrate that DT-ICU delivers accurate, temporally robust, and interpretable predictions, supporting its potential as a practical digital twin framework for continuous patient monitoring in critical care. The source code and trained model weights for DT-ICU are publicly available at https://github.com/GUO-W/DT-ICU-release.

</details>


### [212] [Optimal Learning Rate Schedule for Balancing Effort and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07830)
*Valentina Njaradi,Rodrigo Carrasco-Davis,Peter E. Latham,Andrew Saxe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种规范框架，将学习速度控制建模为最优控制问题，推导出最优学习率的闭式解，并展示了如何通过情景记忆机制近似实现最优行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物和人工智能体都需要高效学习，但学习速度需要平衡改进收益与努力、不稳定性和资源消耗的成本。现有研究缺乏一个统一的规范框架来解释学习速度的自我调节机制。

Method: 将学习速度控制形式化为最优控制过程：智能体最大化累积性能同时承担学习成本。推导出最优学习率的闭式解，该解依赖于当前和预期未来性能。引入情景记忆机制来近似性能期望。

Result: 获得了最优学习率的闭式控制器形式，该解在任务和架构间具有普适性。在简单学习模型中分析了参数如何影响学习率调度。展示了情景记忆可以近似实现最优行为。

Conclusion: 该框架为学习速度控制提供了规范和生物学合理的解释，将自我调节学习、努力分配和情景记忆估计统一在一个可处理的数学框架中，解释了过度自信/自信不足对学习参与的影响。

Abstract: Learning how to learn efficiently is a fundamental challenge for biological agents and a growing concern for artificial ones. To learn effectively, an agent must regulate its learning speed, balancing the benefits of rapid improvement against the costs of effort, instability, or resource use. We introduce a normative framework that formalizes this problem as an optimal control process in which the agent maximizes cumulative performance while incurring a cost of learning. From this objective, we derive a closed-form solution for the optimal learning rate, which has the form of a closed-loop controller that depends only on the agent's current and expected future performance. Under mild assumptions, this solution generalizes across tasks and architectures and reproduces numerically optimized schedules in simulations. In simple learning models, we can mathematically analyze how agent and task parameters shape learning-rate scheduling as an open-loop control solution. Because the optimal policy depends on expectations of future performance, the framework predicts how overconfidence or underconfidence influence engagement and persistence, linking the control of learning speed to theories of self-regulated learning. We further show how a simple episodic memory mechanism can approximate the required performance expectations by recalling similar past learning experiences, providing a biologically plausible route to near-optimal behaviour. Together, these results provide a normative and biologically plausible account of learning speed control, linking self-regulated learning, effort allocation, and episodic memory estimation within a unified and tractable mathematical framework.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [Multi-fidelity constraints in blackbox optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06321)
*Xavier Lebeuf,Stéphane Alarie,Charles Audet,Miguel Diago,Sébastien Le Digabel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该研究提出中断式直接搜索算法（IDS和DIDS），利用多保真度约束评估来加速昂贵黑箱优化问题求解，特别适用于工业应用中计算成本高昂且约束可在不同保真度下评估的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业应用中黑箱优化问题因计算成本过高而难以在合理时间内求解，但约束条件可以在不同保真度下以较低成本评估。现有方法未充分利用多保真度约束信息，特别是当存在大量离散保真度且问题高度不连续时。

Method: 提出两种算法：中断式直接搜索（IDS）和动态中断式直接搜索（DIDS）。这些算法利用多保真度约束评估，在低保真度检测到不可行性时中断昂贵评估，避免不必要的高成本计算。算法基于直接搜索方法，适用于高度不连续问题。

Result: 实验结果表明，当NOMAD（非线性优化软件包）与IDS或DIDS结合时，性能显著提升。新算法能够有效利用多保真度约束信息，减少昂贵评估次数，加速优化过程。

Conclusion: IDS和DIDS算法通过利用多保真度约束评估，有效解决了昂贵黑箱优化问题。这些方法特别适用于工业应用，其中约束可在不同保真度下评估，且问题可能高度不连续。与NOMAD结合使用时，性能得到显著改善。

Abstract: This work studies constrained blackbox optimization problems that cannot be solved in reasonable time due to prohibitive computational costs. This challenge is especially prevalent in industrial applications, where blackbox evaluations are costly. However, constraints can be evaluated at various fidelities at a lower computational cost. More specifically, this work targets situations in which the infeasibility of each individual constraint can be detected at lower fidelities, and where a large discrete number of fidelities are available. Moreover, highly discontinuous problems which may fail to evaluate are considered, such that direct search methods are preferred to model-based ones. To this effect, the Interruptible Direct Search (IDS) and the Dynamic Interruptible Direct Search (DIDS) algorithms are proposed to leverage feasibility assessments from various fidelity levels to avoid high cost evaluations. The results show highly increased performances from NOMAD when it is paired with IDS or DIDS.

</details>


### [214] [On a Gradient Approach to Chebyshev Center Problems with Applications to Function Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06434)
*Abhinav Raghuvanshi,Mayank Baranwal,Debasish Chatterjee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: gradOL是首个基于梯度的优化框架，用于解决切比雪夫中心问题，通过将半无限问题重构为有限的最大-最小优化，利用自动微分实现数值稳定性和可扩展性，在强凸范数条件下可恢复最优切比雪夫中心并计算相关半径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 切比雪夫中心问题是函数学习和几何优化的基础挑战，现有方法在构建稳定最优插值时存在瓶颈，需要一种能够处理大规模设置且具有理论保证的梯度优化框架。

Method: 将半无限问题重构为有限的最大-最小优化问题，利用自动微分进行精确数值梯度计算，确保数值稳定性和可扩展性，适用于大规模设置。

Result: 在34个基准切比雪夫中心问题上取得显著精度和效率提升；扩展到一般凸半无限规划，在67个基准问题上比SIPAMPL求解器快达4000倍；为梯度方法应用于切比雪夫中心问题提供了首个理论基础。

Conclusion: gradOL为切比雪夫中心和更广泛的凸半无限规划提供了统一的解决方案框架，将严格分析与实用算法相结合，解决了构建稳定最优插值的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: We introduce $\textsf{gradOL}$, the first gradient-based optimization framework for solving Chebyshev center problems, a fundamental challenge in optimal function learning and geometric optimization. $\textsf{gradOL}$ hinges on reformulating the semi-infinite problem as a finitary max-min optimization, making it amenable to gradient-based techniques. By leveraging automatic differentiation for precise numerical gradient computation, $\textsf{gradOL}$ ensures numerical stability and scalability, making it suitable for large-scale settings. Under strong convexity of the ambient norm, $\textsf{gradOL}$ provably recovers optimal Chebyshev centers while directly computing the associated radius. This addresses a key bottleneck in constructing stable optimal interpolants. Empirically, $\textsf{gradOL}$ achieves significant improvements in accuracy and efficiency on 34 benchmark Chebyshev center problems from a benchmark $\textsf{CSIP}$ library. Moreover, we extend $\textsf{gradOL}$ to general convex semi-infinite programming (CSIP), attaining up to $4000\times$ speedups over the state-of-the-art $\texttt{SIPAMPL}$ solver tested on the indicated $\textsf{CSIP}$ library containing 67 benchmark problems. Furthermore, we provide the first theoretical foundation for applying gradient-based methods to Chebyshev center problems, bridging rigorous analysis with practical algorithms. $\textsf{gradOL}$ thus offers a unified solution framework for Chebyshev centers and broader CSIPs.

</details>


### [215] [Resource-constrained Project Scheduling with Time-of-Use Energy Tariffs and Machine States: A Logic-based Benders Decomposition Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06542)
*Corentin Juvigny,Antonín Novák,Jan Mandík,Zdeněk Hanzálek*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了考虑分时电价和机器状态的资源受限项目调度问题，提出两种新方法：单一约束规划方法和逻辑Benders分解方法，后者在中等能耗任务比例下表现优异，可处理多达1600个任务的稀疏实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统资源受限项目调度问题未充分考虑能源成本优化，特别是存在高能耗机器和分时电价（包括负电价）的情况。需要开发高效算法来同时最小化项目完成时间和总能源成本。

Method: 提出两种方法：1) 单一约束规划方法；2) 逻辑Benders分解方法，该方法将主问题（处理能源成本的整数线性规划）与子问题（处理RCPSP的约束规划）相结合。

Result: 两种方法均优于传统的紧凑整数线性规划方法。逻辑Benders分解在能耗任务比例适中的情况下显著优于单一约束规划方法，能够解决多达1600个任务的稀疏实例。

Conclusion: 逻辑Benders分解方法为解决考虑分时电价和机器状态的RCPSP提供了有效方案，且该方法可推广到具有类似特征的其他调度问题，如带阻塞时间和总加权延迟的RCPSP以及柔性作业车间问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the Resource-Constrained Project Scheduling Problem (RCPSP) with time-of-use energy tariffs (TOU) and machine states, a variant of RCPSP for production scheduling where energy price is part of the criteria and one machine is highly energy-demanding and can be in one of the following three states: proc, idle, or off. The problem involves scheduling all tasks, respecting precedence constraints and resource limitations, while minimizing the combination of the overall makespan and the total energy cost (TEC), which varies according to the TOU pricing, which can take negative values. We propose two novel approaches to solve it: a monolithic Constraint Programming (CP) approach and a Logic-Based Benders Decomposition (LBBD) approach. The latter combines a master problem dealing with energy cost solved using Integer Linear Programming (ILP) with a subproblem handling the RCPSP resolved using CP. Both approaches surpass the monolithic compact ILP approach, but the LBBD significantly outperforms the CP when the ratio of energy-intensive tasks over the overall tasks is moderate, allowing for solving instances with up to 1600 tasks in sparse instances. Finally, we put forth a way of generalizing our LBBD approach to other problems sharing similar characteristics, and we applied it to a problem based on an RCPSP problem with blocking times & total weighted tardiness criterion and a flexible job shop.

</details>


### [216] [Inexact DC Algorithms in Hilbert Spaces with Applications to PDE-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06622)
*P. D. Khanh,V. V. H. Khoa,B. S. Mordukhovich,D. B. Tran,N. V. Vo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出I-ADCA算法处理希尔伯特空间中DC函数最小化问题，允许不精确次梯度计算和子问题求解，应用于椭圆最优控制问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决希尔伯特空间中DC函数最小化问题，传统DCA算法需要精确计算，实际应用中存在计算困难，需要允许不精确计算的自适应算法

Method: 提出I-ADCA（不精确自适应DC算法），允许不精确次梯度评估和凸子问题的不精确解，在Polyak-Lojasiewicz条件下获得显式收敛速率

Result: 算法保证全局收敛到稳定点，应用于椭圆最优控制问题（含控制约束和非凸L^{1-2}稀疏正则项），通过有限元离散化建立适定性和误差界估计

Conclusion: I-ADCA是处理DC优化问题的有效算法，特别适用于非凸稀疏正则化的椭圆最优控制问题，数值实验验证了理论结果

Abstract: In this paper, we design and apply novel inexact adaptive algorithms to deal with minimizing difference-of-convex (DC) functions in Hilbert spaces. We first introduce I-ADCA, an inexact adaptive counterpart of the well-recognized DCA (difference-of-convex algorithm), that allows inexact subgradient evaluations and inexact solutions to convex subproblems while still guarantees global convergence to stationary points. Under a Polyak-Lojasiewicz type property for DC objectives, we obtain explicit convergence rates for the proposed algorithm. Our main application addresses elliptic optimal control problems with control constraints and nonconvex $L^{1-2}$ sparsity-enhanced regularizers admitting a DC decomposition. Employing I-ADCA and appropriate versions of finite element discretization leads us to an efficient procedure for solving such problems with establishing its well-posedness and error bound estimates confirmed by numerical experiments.

</details>


### [217] [A finite-termination algorithm for testing copositivity over the positive semidefinite cone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06648)
*Lei Huang,Lingling Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种测试齐次多项式在正半定锥上余正性的高效算法，基于矩阵优化重构，通过求解一系列半定规划，有限步终止，能提供证书或反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要高效测试齐次多项式在正半定锥上的余正性，这是优化和组合优化中的重要问题，现有方法可能计算复杂或无法保证有限步终止。

Method: 基于新颖的矩阵优化重构，通过求解一系列半定规划（SDP）层次结构，算法总是有限步终止。如果多项式是余正的，提供证书；否则返回反驳余正性的向量。类似思想扩展到正半定锥与非负正交直积上的余正性测试。

Result: 算法能有效测试齐次多项式在正半定锥上的余正性，有限步终止，提供证书或反例。初步数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种高效、有限步终止的算法来测试齐次多项式在正半定锥上的余正性，并扩展到更一般的锥上，数值实验表明方法有效。

Abstract: This paper proposes an efficient algorithm for testing copositivity of homogeneous polynomials over the positive semidefinite cone. The algorithm is based on a novel matrix optimization reformulation and requires solving a hierarchy of semidefinite programs. Notably, it always terminates in finitely many iterations. If a homogeneous polynomial is copositive over the positive semidefinite cone, the algorithm provides a certificate; otherwise, it returns a vector that refutes copositivity. Building on a similar idea, we further propose an algorithm to test copositivity over the direct product of the positive semidefinite cone and the nonnegative orthant. Preliminary numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [218] [Splitting Proximal Point Algorithms for the Sum of Prox-Convex Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06970)
*Jose de Brito,Felipe Lara,Tran Van Thang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将分裂近端点算法从凸函数推广到近凸函数，提出确定性和随机性两种变体，证明了在标准步长条件下的全局收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决有限个近凸函数之和的最小化问题，其中每个分量函数满足Lipschitz连续性。现有分裂近端点算法主要针对凸函数，需要将其扩展到更广泛的近凸函数类。

Method: 提出了两种分裂近端点算法变体：1）确定性变体，采用固定更新顺序；2）随机变体，采用随机采样。将Bacak和Bertsekas提出的算法从凸函数扩展到近凸函数。

Result: 在标准步长假设下证明了两种方法的全局收敛性，其中随机变体通过超鞅理论证明了几乎必然收敛。使用非凸二次函数的数值实验验证了方法的效率和理论结果。

Conclusion: 成功将分裂近端点算法扩展到近凸函数优化问题，提出的确定性和随机性算法在理论和实验上都表现出良好的性能，为解决更广泛的非凸优化问题提供了有效工具。

Abstract: This paper addresses the minimization of a finite sum of prox-convex functions under Lipschitz continuity of each component. We propose two variants of the splitting proximal point algorithms proposed in \cite{Bacak,Bertsekas}: one deterministic with a fixed update order, and one stochastic with random sampling, and we extend them from convex to prox-convex functions. We prove global convergence for both methods under standard stepsize a\-ssump\-tions, with almost sure convergence for the stochastic variant via supermartingale theory. Numerical experiments with nonconvex quadratic functions illustrate the efficiency of the proposed methods and support the theoretical results.

</details>


### [219] [A belief-state restless bandit model for treatment adherence: Whittle indexability via partial conservation laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06976)
*José Niño-Mora,Ángel Pellitero García*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究容量受限的治疗依从性外展问题，使用信念状态不安定多臂老虎机模型，患者为部分可观测的二状态（依从/不依从）马尔可夫过程，干预具有重置型信念动态。通过部分守恒定律证明单患者问题的Whittle可索引性，推导闭式Whittle指数和阈值策略性能指标，提供高效计算多患者对偶界和最优性间隙的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究容量受限的治疗依从性外展问题，需要优化有限干预资源在患者群体中的分配，以最大化长期依从性。患者依从状态是部分可观测的，且干预具有重置效应，这构成了一个具有特殊动态的随机优化问题。

Method: 采用信念状态不安定多臂老虎机模型，患者建模为部分可观测的二状态马尔可夫过程。使用部分守恒定律分析单患者问题，证明Whittle可索引性，推导闭式Whittle指数和阈值策略性能指标。建立解析拉格朗日松弛，将单患者拉格朗日值表示为干预价格的逐段仿射凸函数，实现高效多患者对偶界计算。

Result: 1) 证明单患者问题的Whittle可索引性；2) 推导闭式Whittle指数和阈值策略性能指标；3) 建立解析拉格朗日松弛方法，实现高效对偶界计算；4) 在大规模数值研究中，Whittle指数策略在容量紧张且存在脆弱少数群体时表现优异，相比近视策略获得高达26%的奖励提升和更小的相对最优性间隙。

Conclusion: 该研究为容量受限的治疗依从性外展问题提供了理论分析和实用算法。Whittle指数策略在特定场景下（容量紧张且存在脆弱少数群体）具有显著优势，而近视策略在多数情况下具有竞争力。方法能够计算认证的相对最优性间隙，为实际决策提供理论保证。

Abstract: We study capacity-constrained treatment-adherence outreach via a belief-state restless multi-armed bandit model where patients are a partially observed two-state (adherent/nonadherent) Markov processes and interventions induce reset-type belief dynamics. Using partial conservation laws (PCLs), we establish Whittle indexability of the single-patient problem and derive a closed-form Whittle (marginal productivity) index, together with closed-form reward/work performance metrics under threshold policies and an explicit optimal threshold map. This yields an analytic Lagrangian relaxation: the single-patient Lagrangian value is a piecewise-affine convex function of the intervention price, enabling efficient computation of multi-patient dual bounds and certified relative optimality gaps. We also analyze how the Whittle index depends on the lapse and spontaneous-recovery parameters, providing qualitative insights on intervention priorities. In a large-scale numerical study over heterogeneous two-type populations, we compare Whittle's index policy with a myopic index rule and simple baselines; while myopic is highly competitive on most instances, Whittle's policy yields substantial gains in tight-capacity regimes with a fragile minority, reaching up to about $26\%$ higher reward and markedly smaller relative optimality gaps.

</details>


### [220] [Policy stability and ultimate stationarity in discounted risk-sensitive stochastic control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06998)
*Nicole Bäuerle,Marcin Pitera,Łukasz Stettner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究有限状态-动作空间离散时间MDP中长程折扣风险敏感目标的稳定性，分析风险厌恶参数和折扣因子扰动下的鲁棒性、最终平稳性出现，以及折扣与平均公式在混合假设下的相互作用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究风险敏感MDP中策略和值函数的稳定性，特别是在风险厌恶参数和折扣因子扰动下的鲁棒性，以及理解折扣风险敏感目标与平均风险敏感目标之间的关系

Method: 分析离散时间MDP在有限状态-动作空间上的长程折扣风险敏感目标，研究参数扰动下的稳定性、极限状态（折扣消失和风险敏感消失）、Blackwell型稳定性性质，并提供数值示例

Result: 揭示了最优折扣风险敏感策略的内在非平稳性，分析了风险厌恶参数和折扣因子扰动下的稳定性，探讨了折扣与平均公式在混合假设下的相互作用关系

Conclusion: 最优折扣风险敏感策略具有内在非平稳性，但在特定条件下会出现最终平稳性；参数扰动下的稳定性分析为风险敏感MDP的鲁棒性提供了理论依据，极限状态分析连接了折扣与平均风险敏感目标

Abstract: We study discrete-time Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) on finite state-action spaces and analyze the stability of optimal policies and value functions in the long-run discounted risk-sensitive objective setting. Our analysis addresses robustness with respect to perturbations of the risk-aversion parameter and the discount factor, the emergence of ultimate stationarity, and the interaction between discounted and averaged formulations under suitable mixing assumptions. We further investigate limiting regimes associated with vanishing discount and vanishing risk sensitivity, and discuss the role of Blackwell-type stability properties in the discounted setting. Finally, we provide numerical illustrations that highlight the intrinsic non-stationarity of optimal discounted risk-sensitive policies.

</details>


### [221] [The Güntürk-Thao theorem revisited: polyhedral cones and limiting examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07002)
*Heinz H. Bauschke,Tran Thanh Tung*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文将Güntürk和Thao关于有限个固有正则闭子空间随机投影序列收敛性的结果扩展到有限个多面锥的情况，并构造反例证明扩展的紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Güntürk和Thao在2023年证明了在实Hilbert空间中，从有限个固有正则闭子空间生成的随机（松弛）投影序列满足∑‖x^(n)-x^(n+1)‖^γ<∞对所有γ>0成立。作者希望将这一结果扩展到更一般的多面锥集合，并探究扩展的边界条件。

Method: 采用数学分析方法，首先将Güntürk和Thao的结果从固有正则闭子空间扩展到有限个多面锥。然后通过构造反例来验证扩展的紧致性：在ℝ²中构造直线和凸集的例子，在ℝ³中构造平面和非多面锥的例子，证明在这些情况下原结果不再成立。

Result: 成功将原结果扩展到有限个多面锥的情况，证明了随机投影序列仍然满足∑‖x^(n)-x^(n+1)‖^γ<∞对所有γ>0成立。同时通过构造的反例表明：当集合包含非多面结构时（如ℝ²中的直线和凸集，或ℝ³中的平面和非多面锥），该收敛性质可能失效。

Conclusion: 该研究将随机投影序列的收敛性结果从固有正则闭子空间推广到多面锥，并通过反例精确界定了推广的有效范围，揭示了多面性在这一收敛性质中的关键作用。

Abstract: In 2023, Güntürk and Thao proved that the sequence $(x^{(n)})_{n\in\mathbb{N}}$ generated by random (relaxed) projections drawn from a finite collection of innately regular closed subspaces in a real Hilbert space satisfies $\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}} \|x^{(n)}-x^{(n+1)}\|^γ<+\infty$ for all $γ>0$.
  We extend their result to a finite collection of polyhedral cones. Moreover, we construct examples showing the tightness of our extension: indeed, the result fails for a line and a convex set in $\mathbb{R}^2$, and for a plane and a non-polyhedral cone in $\mathbb{R}^3$.

</details>


### [222] [Primal-Dual algorithms for Abstract convex functions with respect to quadratic functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07076)
*Ewa Bednarczuk,The Hung Tran*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出针对抽象凸目标函数的鞍点问题的原始-对偶算法，使用抽象邻近算子，在特定限制下研究收敛性，并通过数值实验验证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决目标函数相对于二次函数类为抽象凸的鞍点问题，这类问题在优化中具有广泛的应用背景，需要开发有效的算法来处理这种特殊的凸性结构

Method: 提出基于抽象邻近算子的原始-对偶算法，利用目标函数相对于二次函数类的抽象凸性质，在特定限制条件下设计收敛算法

Result: 算法在特定限制下具有收敛性，通过多个数值例子验证了算法的有效性和性能

Conclusion: 成功开发了处理抽象凸鞍点问题的原始-对偶算法，证明了在适当条件下的收敛性，并通过数值实验展示了算法的实用性

Abstract: We consider the saddle point problem where the objective functions are abstract convex with respect to the class of quadratic functions. We propose primal-dual algorithms using the corresponding abstract proximal operator and investigate the convergence under certain restrictions. We test our algorithms by several numerical examples.

</details>


### [223] [Adaptive Robust Control for Uncertain Systems with Ellipsoid-Set Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07079)
*Xuehui Ma,Shiliang Zhang,Zhiyong Sun,Xiaohui Zhang,Sabita Maharjan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种针对线性不确定系统的自适应鲁棒控制方法，使用椭球集而非高斯分布描述过程噪声、观测噪声和系统状态，通过椭球集学习估计状态集边界并减少鲁棒控制的保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机控制方法在处理非高斯不确定性方面存在局限性，传统方法通常假设高斯分布，无法有效处理更一般的不确定性形式。需要开发能够处理非高斯不确定性的自适应鲁棒控制方法。

Method: 1) 设计椭球集学习方法估计状态集边界；2) 将学习到的集合纳入控制律推导以减少保守性；3) 考虑状态空间矩阵中的参数不确定性，为不确定参数分配有限候选值；4) 为每个候选构建候选条件鲁棒控制问题库；5) 通过聚合候选条件控制律得到最终控制律。

Result: 在数值模拟中验证了所提控制方法的有效性，包括线性二次调节和跟踪控制两种情况。该方法通过并行鲁棒控制分离学习与控制，解决了传统方法中学习与控制耦合导致控制不可行的问题。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应鲁棒控制方法能够有效处理非高斯不确定性，通过椭球集表示和候选条件控制框架，在减少保守性的同时实现了学习与控制的解耦，为线性不确定系统控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: Despite the celebrated success of stochastic control approaches for uncertain systems, such approaches are limited in the ability to handle non-Gaussian uncertainties. This work presents an adaptive robust control for linear uncertain systems, whose process noise, observation noise, and system states are depicted by ellipsoid sets rather than Gaussian distributions. We design an ellipsoid-set learning method to estimate the boundaries of state sets, and incorporate the learned sets into the control law derivation to reduce conservativeness in robust control. Further, we consider the parametric uncertainties in state-space matrices. Particularly, we assign finite candidates for the uncertain parameters, and construct a bank of candidate-conditional robust control problems for each candidate. We derive the final control law by aggregating the candidate-conditional control laws. In this way, we separate the control scheme into parallel robust controls, decoupling the learning and control, which otherwise renders the control unattainable. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed control in numerical simulations in the cases of linear quadratic regulation and tracking control.

</details>


### [224] [Cyclic Modulation Control of Multi-Conflict Connected Automated Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07114)
*Fan Pu,Zihao Li,Sivakumar Rathinam,Minghui Zheng,Yang Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: CMAT是一个几何无关的统一框架，通过将冲突点嵌入重复的"微相位"序列，实现多冲突场景下CAV的协同控制，显著降低延迟并提高吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAV策略局限于特定场景（如高速公路匝道合流或单车道环岛），传统交通信号通过刚性相位和全红间隔牺牲效率换取安全。多冲突交通普遍存在，需要一种统一、几何无关的框架来处理任意合并、分流和交叉模式。

Method: CMAT将每个冲突点嵌入重复的"微相位"序列，车辆动态形成需求响应型车队，协商占用冲突点的时间槽。该方法基于物理交叉口布局抽象的有向图，构建混合整数线性规划模型，实现无碰撞通行和高交叉口利用率。

Result: CMAT在多种多冲突测试场景（包括简单双向交叉、四路交叉口、复杂连接交叉口）中表现出色，相比最先进的CAV协调方法和传统信号配时策略，显著降低了延迟并大幅提高了吞吐量。

Conclusion: CMAT提供了一个统一、几何无关的框架，能够处理任意多冲突模式，最小化延迟，保证安全，无需手动重新调整，为多冲突场景下的CAV协同控制提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-conflict traffic is ubiquitous. Connected Automated Vehicles (CAVs) offer unprecedented opportunities to enhance safety, reduce emissions, and increase throughput through precise coordination and automation. However, existing CAV strategies remain confined to specialized scenarios, such as highway on-ramp merging or single-lane roundabouts, and traditional traffic signals sacrifice efficiency for safety via rigid phasing and all-red intervals. In this paper, we present Cyclic Modulation Control of Multi-Conflict Connected Automated Traffic (CMAT), a unified, geometry-agnostic framework that embeds each conflict point into a repeating sequence of "micro-phases". Vehicles dynamically form platoons with demand-responsive sizes and negotiate time slots for occupying conflict points, enabling collision-free traversal and high intersection utilization. CMAT aims to minimize delay, guarantee safety, and accommodate arbitrary merging, diverging, and crossing patterns without manual retuning. We formalize CMAT as a mixed-integer linear programming model constructed on a directed graph abstracted from the physical intersection layout. The performance of CMAT is evaluated across a suite of multi-conflict tests, including simple two-way crossings, four-leg intersections, complex connected intersections. The results demonstrate substantial reductions in delay and significant throughput improvements compared with state-of-the-art CAV coordination methods and traditional signal timing strategies.

</details>


### [225] [Nonquadratic global asymptotic stability certificates for saturated linear feedbacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07431)
*Andrea Cristofaro,Luca Zaccarian*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出非二次Lyapunov函数正定性的充分条件，用于分析带饱和反馈的线性系统全局渐近稳定性，特别针对ANCBI类不可全局指数镇定系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对带饱和反馈的线性系统，特别是那些无法全局指数镇定的ANCBI类系统，需要建立非保守的稳定性分析方法。传统二次Lyapunov函数方法存在局限性，需要发展非二次Lyapunov函数理论

Method: 构建由状态和输入死区函数组成的符号不定二次形式的非二次Lyapunov函数，建立正定/半正定的充分条件，利用不变性原理证明全局渐近稳定性。针对多输入系统，将稳定性分析转化为线性矩阵不等式形式的凸优化问题

Result: 所提条件对ANCBI类原型系统是非保守的（充分必要条件），多输入扩展可得到适用于ANCBI不可全局指数镇定系统的凸稳定性分析测试

Conclusion: 成功建立了非二次Lyapunov函数理论框架，为带饱和反馈的线性系统，特别是ANCBI类不可全局指数镇定系统，提供了有效的全局渐近稳定性分析方法

Abstract: We establish sufficient conditions for positive (semi-)definiteness, with or without radial unboundedness, for nonquadratic Lyapunov function constructed as sign-indefinite quadratic forms involving the state and the deadzone of a suitable input. We then use these conditions to build weak nonquadratic Lyapunov functions establishing global asymptotic stability of linear systems in feedback through a saturation, leveraging invariance principles. Our results are shown to be non-conservative (necessary and sufficient) for a family of well known prototypical examples of linear SISO feedbacks that are not globally exponentially stabilizable (the so-called ANCBI plants). Our multi-input extension leads to convex stability analysis tests, formulated as linear matrix inequalities that are applicable to ANCBI non-globally-exponentially-stabilizable plants.

</details>


### [226] [Design of Optimal Controls in Acausal LQG Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07433)
*Arzu Ahmadova,Agamirza E. Bashirov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统化整理了非因果系统的相关结果，为设计非因果LQG问题的最优控制提供了理论基础


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制理论中大多数文献讨论因果系统，只有少数间接涉及非因果系统，缺乏系统化的理论框架

Method: 系统化整理非因果系统的现有结果，构建非因果LQG问题的最优控制设计背景

Result: 建立了非因果系统理论的系统化框架，为非因果LQG问题的最优控制设计提供了理论基础

Conclusion: 非因果系统在控制理论中具有重要意义，本文填补了该领域系统化理论框架的空白

Abstract: In control theory, a system which has output depending only on the present and past values of the input is said to be causal (or nonanticipative). Respectively, a system is acausal (or non-causal) if its output depends on future inputs as well. Overall majority of literature in stochastic control theory discusses causal systems. Only a few sources indirectly concern acausal systems. In this paper, we systemize these results under main idea of acausality and present a background for designing optimal controls in acausal LQG problems.

</details>


### [227] [Online Markov Decision Processes with Terminal Law Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07492)
*Bianca Marin Moreno,Margaux Brégère,Pierre Gaillard,Nadia Oudjane*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出周期性框架用于无重置强化学习，通过寻找周期性策略（在固定步数后返回初始状态分布）来平衡累积损失和终端约束，为多智能体场景提供首个非渐进保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习通常假设有重置的片段式交互或连续操作最小化平均/累积损失。片段式设置虽有理论结果但重置在实践中不现实，无限时域设置避免了重置问题但在未知动态的在线场景中缺乏非渐进保证。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 引入周期性框架，目标是寻找周期性策略：不仅最小化累积损失，还在固定步数后将智能体返回初始状态分布。形式化寻找最优周期性策略的问题，为表格MDP确定充分条件。引入周期性遗憾作为评估指标，平衡累积损失与终端分布约束。提出首个计算周期性策略的算法，适用于两种多智能体设置。

Result: 提出的算法在两种多智能体设置中实现了亚线性周期性遗憾 $\tilde O(T^{3/4})$，为 $M > 1$ 的同质智能体提供了首个无重置学习的非渐进保证。

Conclusion: 周期性框架为无重置强化学习提供了理论框架，通过周期性策略平衡长期性能和状态分布约束，为多智能体场景提供了首个非渐进性能保证，填补了传统片段式与无限时域设置之间的空白。

Abstract: Traditional reinforcement learning usually assumes either episodic interactions with resets or continuous operation to minimize average or cumulative loss. While episodic settings have many theoretical results, resets are often unrealistic in practice. The infinite-horizon setting avoids this issue but lacks non-asymptotic guarantees in online scenarios with unknown dynamics. In this work, we move towards closing this gap by introducing a reset-free framework called the periodic framework, where the goal is to find periodic policies: policies that not only minimize cumulative loss but also return the agents to their initial state distribution after a fixed number of steps. We formalize the problem of finding optimal periodic policies and identify sufficient conditions under which it is well-defined for tabular Markov decision processes. To evaluate algorithms in this framework, we introduce the periodic regret, a measure that balances cumulative loss with the terminal law constraint. We then propose the first algorithms for computing periodic policies in two multi-agent settings and show they achieve sublinear periodic regret of order $\tilde O(T^{3/4})$. This provides the first non-asymptotic guarantees for reset-free learning in the setting of $M$ homogeneous agents, for $M > 1$.

</details>


### [228] [Data-Driven Stochastic VRP: Integration of Forecast Duration into Optimization for Utility Workforce Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07514)
*Matteo Garbelli*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将机器学习预测的干预持续时间集成到带时间窗的随机容量车辆路径问题中，通过XGBoost预测和不确定性估计驱动多目标进化优化，提升运营效率20-25%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现实世界车辆路径规划中服务持续时间的不确定性问题，传统方法使用固定持续时间无法适应实际变化，需要集成机器学习预测和不确定性量化来优化运营效率

Method: 使用XGBoost基于8年燃气表维护数据进行点预测和不确定性估计，通过次高斯集中界限构建路线级风险缓冲，采用多目标进化优化算法平衡竞争性运营KPI

Result: 相比使用默认持续时间的规划，操作员利用率和完成率提升约20-25%，预测残差分析验证了次高斯假设的有效性

Conclusion: 机器学习预测与不确定性量化结合风险感知优化为处理现实世界路由应用中的随机服务持续时间提供了实用框架，显著改善运营效率

Abstract: This paper investigates the integration of machine learning forecasts of intervention durations into a stochastic variant of the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (CVRPTW). In particular, we exploit tree-based gradient boosting (XGBoost) trained on eight years of gas meter maintenance data to produce point predictions and uncertainty estimates, which then drive a multi-objective evolutionary optimization routine. The methodology addresses uncertainty through sub-Gaussian concentration bounds for route-level risk buffers and explicitly accounts for competing operational KPIs through a multi-objective formulation. Empirical analysis of prediction residuals validates the sub-Gaussian assumption underlying the risk model. From an empirical point of view, our results report improvements around 20-25\% in operator utilization and completion rates compared with plans computed using default durations. The integration of uncertainty quantification and risk-aware optimization provides a practical framework for handling stochastic service durations in real-world routing applications.

</details>


### [229] [Dually cone-boundedness of a set and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07517)
*Marius Durea,Elena-Cristina Stamate*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文引入并研究了一种基于锥的向量空间子集有界性广义概念，通过锥的所有正泛函来定义，证明了这是比现有概念更弱的有界性，并展示了其在推广重要结果和技巧中的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中关于向量空间子集有界性的概念存在局限性，需要一种更广义、更灵活的有界性定义，以便更好地处理锥结构下的数学问题，并为推广重要数学结果提供理论基础。

Method: 通过锥的所有正泛函来定义子集的有界性（即子集在所有正泛函下的像都是下有界的），将这种新定义与现有类似概念进行比较分析，探索其与现有文献的联系，并基于此概念推广重要数学结果和技巧。

Result: 证明了这种基于锥正泛函的有界性定义是比现有类似概念更弱的有界性概念，建立了与现有文献的多个联系，并成功地将该概念应用于推广锥消去法则和Rådström嵌入过程等重要数学结果和技巧。

Conclusion: 提出的基于锥正泛函的有界性概念为处理锥结构下的数学问题提供了更灵活的工具，能够有效推广多个重要数学结果，具有重要的理论价值和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: We introduce and study a generalized concept of boundedness of a subset of a normed vector space with respect to a cone, which is defined as lower boundedness of the images of the underlying set through all the positive functionals of the cone. We show that this is a weaker notion when compared to other similar ones and we explore several links with the existing literature. We subsequently demonstrate that this concept furnishes the properties required to obtain various generalizations of important results and techniques, including conic cancellation rules and the Rådström embedding procedure.

</details>


### [230] [Speaking of Opinions: Comparing Approaches to Modelling Opinion Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07619)
*Luisa Estrada,Sasha Glendinning,Andrew Nugent*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文综述了数学和计算机科学中意见形成与操纵建模的主要方法，比较了连续意见空间和离散/二元意见模型的优缺点，并提供了跨学科视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在系统梳理数学和计算机科学领域中意见形成与操纵建模的各种方法，为研究人员提供该领域的全面概览，促进不同建模方法的比较和理解。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，将模型分为连续意见空间模型和离散/二元意见模型两大类。介绍了常微分方程、偏微分方程、随机模型、控制理论和交互协议等关键工具，并比较了这些方法在描述操纵方面的应用。

Result: 系统整理了意见动力学领域的主要建模方法，分析了连续和离散意见模型的各自优势与局限性，为研究者选择合适模型提供了参考框架。

Conclusion: 意见动力学是一个重要的跨学科研究领域，具有显著的社会意义。通过数学和计算模型可以更好地理解意见形成和操纵机制，未来需要更多跨学科合作来推动该领域发展。

Abstract: This review outlines the major approaches to modelling opinion formation and manipulation in mathematics and computer science. Key tools such as ordinary and partial differential equations, stochastic models, control theory, and interaction protocols are introduced and compared as methods for describing manipulation. The review is separated into those models using a continuous opinion space and those using discrete or binary opinions, with the advantages and disadvantages of each discussed. Finally, the authors provide an interdisciplinary perspective on the field of opinion dynamics and its social significance.

</details>


### [231] [Beyond Single-GPU: Scaling PDLP to Distributed Multi-GPU Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07628)
*Hongpei Li,Yicheng Huang,Huikang Liu,Dongdong Ge,Yinyu Ye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 分布式PDHG算法实现，用于解决大规模线性规划问题，通过多GPU并行克服单GPU内存限制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDHG算法在单节点GPU上表现良好，但在工业规模实例中受限于GPU内存容量和计算吞吐量，需要扩展到分布式内存环境

Method: 1) 通过二维网格划分约束矩阵实现分布式内存扩展；2) 使用NCCL通信后端同步原始-对偶更新；3) 引入块级随机洗牌策略和非零感知数据分布改善负载均衡；4) 通过融合CUDA内核加速计算

Result: 在标准LP基准测试（MIPLIB和Hans实例）以及大规模实际数据集上，基于cuPDLPx的分布式实现展现出强可扩展性和高性能，同时保持FP64数值精度

Conclusion: 提出的分布式框架不仅克服了单GPU内存瓶颈，还通过多GPU并行实现了显著加速，通信开销相对较低，适用于大规模线性规划问题

Abstract: In this work, we present a distributed implementation of the Primal-Dual Hybrid Gradient (PDHG) algorithm for solving massive-scale linear programming (LP) problems. Although PDHG-based solvers have shown strong performance on single-node GPU architectures, their applicability to industrial-scale instances is often limited by GPU memory capacity and computational throughput. To overcome these challenges, we extend the PDHG framework to a distributed-memory setting via a practical two-dimensional grid partitioning of the constraint matrix, enabling scalable execution across multiple GPUs. Our implementation leverages the NCCL communication backend to efficiently synchronize primal-dual updates across devices. To improve load balance and computational efficiency, we introduce a block-wise random shuffling strategy combined with nonzero-aware data distribution, and further accelerate computation through fused CUDA kernels. By distributing both memory and computation, the proposed framework not only overcomes the single-GPU memory bottleneck but also achieves substantial speedups by exploiting multi-GPU parallelism with relatively low communication overhead. Extensive experiments on standard LP benchmarks, including MIPLIB and Hans' instances, as well as large-scale real-world datasets, show that our distributed implementation, built upon cuPDLPx, achieves strong scalability and high performance while preserving full FP64 numerical accuracy.

</details>


### [232] [To report or not to report: Optimal claim reporting in a bonus-malus system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07655)
*Lea Enzi,Stefan Thonhauser*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究保险奖惩系统下的最优索赔报告问题，投保人可决定是否报告已发生索赔，采用屏障型报告策略最大化终端财富期望值


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在奖惩系统保险中，报告索赔会导致保费上升，而保持无索赔记录则可降低保费。投保人面临是否报告已发生索赔的决策权衡，需要找到最优报告策略以最大化最终财富

Method: 将问题建模为最优控制问题，投保人采用屏障型报告策略。通过建立Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程系统，证明价值函数是该系统的唯一粘性解，并基于此计算最优屏障策略的数值近似

Result: 证明了关联价值函数是Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程系统的唯一粘性解，这一特性使得能够计算最优屏障策略的数值近似

Conclusion: 在奖惩系统保险中，投保人可以通过优化索赔报告决策来最大化终端财富，屏障型策略是最优的，且可通过数值方法计算具体的最优报告阈值

Abstract: We study an optimal claim reporting problem in a bonus-malus setting. We assume, that the insurance contract consists of two regimes, where reporting a claim leads to a transition to a higher-premium regime, whereas remaining claim-free for a prespecified time period results in a shift to the lower premium regime. The insured can decide whether or not to report an occurred claim. We formulate this as an optimal control problem, where the policyholder follows a barrier-type reporting strategy, with the goal of maximizing the expected value of a function of their terminal wealth. We show that the associated value function is the unique viscosity solution to a system of Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equations. This characterization allows us to compute numerical approximations of the optimal barrier strategies.

</details>


### [233] [Convergence and turnpike properties of linear-quadratic mean field control problems with common noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07815)
*Erhan Bayraktar,Jiamin Jian*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究具有共同噪声的线性二次平均场控制问题的收敛性和Turnpike性质，分析有限时域社会优化问题、其平均场控制极限及对应的遍历平均场控制问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有共同噪声的线性二次平均场控制问题的收敛性和Turnpike性质，为大规模多智能体系统的优化控制提供理论分析框架

Method: 采用统一框架分析三类问题：有限时域社会优化问题、平均场控制极限、遍历平均场控制问题；有限时域问题通过耦合Riccati微分方程描述，遍历问题通过Wasserstein空间上的Bellman方程处理，简化为稳定的代数Riccati方程组

Result: 通过对Riccati系统的估计，建立了有限时域平均场控制问题的Turnpike性质，获得了从社会优化问题到其平均场极限及相应遍历控制问题的定量收敛结果

Conclusion: 为具有共同噪声的线性二次平均场控制问题提供了收敛性和Turnpike性质的完整理论框架，建立了有限时域问题与遍历问题之间的联系

Abstract: We investigate convergence and turnpike properties for linear-quadratic mean field control problems with common noise. Within a unified framework, we analyze a finite-horizon social optimization problem, its mean field control limit, and the corresponding ergodic mean field control problem. The finite-horizon problems are characterized by coupled Riccati differential equations, whereas the ergodic problem is addressed via a Bellman equation on the Wasserstein space, which reduces to a system of stabilizing algebraic Riccati equations. By deriving estimates for these Riccati systems, we establish a turnpike property for the finite-horizon mean field control problem and obtain quantitative convergence results from the social optimization problem to its mean field limit and the associated ergodic control problem.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [Automatic Question Generation for Intuitive Learning Utilizing Causal Graph Guided Chain of Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06098)
*Nicholas X. Wang,Neel V. Parpia,Aaryan D. Parikh,Aggelos K. Katsaggelos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出结合因果图引导的思维链推理与多智能体LLM架构的新框架，用于生成准确、有意义且与课程对齐的STEM教育问题，显著减少LLM幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直觉学习对STEM教育的深度概念理解至关重要，但现有自动问题生成方法受限于LLM的幻觉问题（生成事实错误、模糊或教学不一致的问题），需要更可靠的问题生成框架。

Method: 结合因果图引导的思维链推理与多智能体LLM架构：1) 因果图提供领域知识的显式表示；2) 思维链推理实现结构化、逐步的概念遍历；3) 专用LLM智能体分别负责图路径查找、推理、验证和输出任务；4) 概念和输出阶段的双重验证机制。

Result: 实验结果显示：1) 与参考方法相比质量提升高达70%；2) 主观评估获得高度有利的结果；3) 双重验证机制显著减少了幻觉问题。

Conclusion: 提出的因果图引导思维链推理与多智能体LLM框架能有效生成准确、有意义且与课程对齐的STEM教育问题，为个性化自适应学习提供了可靠的问题生成解决方案。

Abstract: Intuitive learning is crucial for developing deep conceptual understanding, especially in STEM education, where students often struggle with abstract and interconnected concepts. Automatic question generation has become an effective strategy for personalized and adaptive learning. However, its effectiveness is hindered by hallucinations in large language models (LLMs), which may generate factually incorrect, ambiguous, or pedagogically inconsistent questions. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework that combines causal-graph-guided Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning with a multi-agent LLM architecture. This approach ensures the generation of accurate, meaningful, and curriculum-aligned questions. Causal graphs provide an explicit representation of domain knowledge, while CoT reasoning facilitates a structured, step-by-step traversal of related concepts. Dedicated LLM agents are assigned specific tasks such as graph pathfinding, reasoning, validation, and output, all working within domain constraints. A dual validation mechanism-at both the conceptual and output stages-greatly reduces hallucinations. Experimental results demonstrate up to a 70% improvement in quality compared to reference methods and yielded highly favorable outcomes in subjective evaluations.

</details>


### [235] [Dynamic Intelligence Ceilings: Measuring Long-Horizon Limits of Planning and Creativity in Artificial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06102)
*Truong Xuan Khanh,Truong Quynh Hoa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"动态智能上限"概念，通过轨迹中心评估框架衡量智能系统的持续发展能力，而非静态性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统在获得显著性能提升的同时，存在过早固定性能边界的问题，缺乏长期发展能力，需要新的评估框架来衡量智能系统的持续成长潜力。

Method: 提出动态智能上限概念，建立轨迹中心评估框架，使用渐进难度上限和上限漂移率两个估计器，通过程序生成基准测试环境来联合评估长期规划和结构创造力。

Result: 研究揭示了在固定解流形内深化开发与随时间持续扩展边界系统之间的质性区别，表明智能边界是动态且轨迹依赖的，而非静态和过早固定的。

Conclusion: 智能系统的限制应被重新定义为动态和轨迹依赖的，而非静态和过早固定的，动态智能上限框架为评估AI系统的持续发展能力提供了新视角。

Abstract: Recent advances in artificial intelligence have produced systems capable of remarkable performance across a wide range of tasks. These gains, however, are increasingly accompanied by concerns regarding long-horizon developmental behavior, as many systems converge toward repetitive solution patterns rather than sustained growth.
  We argue that a central limitation of contemporary AI systems lies not in capability per se, but in the premature fixation of their performance frontier. To address this issue, we introduce the concept of a \emph{Dynamic Intelligence Ceiling} (DIC), defined as the highest level of effective intelligence attainable by a system at a given time under its current resources, internal intent, and structural configuration.
  To make this notion empirically tractable, we propose a trajectory-centric evaluation framework that measures intelligence as a moving frontier rather than a static snapshot. We operationalize DIC using two estimators: the \emph{Progressive Difficulty Ceiling} (PDC), which captures the maximal reliably solvable difficulty under constrained resources, and the \emph{Ceiling Drift Rate} (CDR), which quantifies the temporal evolution of this frontier. These estimators are instantiated through a procedurally generated benchmark that jointly evaluates long-horizon planning and structural creativity within a single controlled environment.
  Our results reveal a qualitative distinction between systems that deepen exploitation within a fixed solution manifold and those that sustain frontier expansion over time. Importantly, our framework does not posit unbounded intelligence, but reframes limits as dynamic and trajectory-dependent rather than static and prematurely fixed.
  \vspace{0.5em} \noindent\textbf{Keywords:} AI evaluation, planning and creativity, developmental intelligence, dynamic intelligence ceilings, complex adaptive systems

</details>


### [236] [Comment on arXiv:2511.21731v1: Identifying Quantum Structure in AI Language: Evidence for Evolutionary Convergence of Human and Artificial Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06104)
*Krzysztof Sienicki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对arXiv:2511.21731v1的技术性检查，指出该论文在CHSH/Bell型计算和Bose-Einstein拟合方面的解释超出了方法支持范围，并指出"能级间距"类比中的内部不一致性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对arXiv:2511.21731v1进行建设性的技术检查，旨在澄清该论文中关于量子纠缠的解读，特别是当"能量"由排名定义时，明确哪些结论是方法支持的，哪些是过度解读

Method: 技术性检查方法包括：1) 分析论文对CHSH/Bell型计算的解释是否超出方法支持范围；2) 检查Bose-Einstein拟合到排名-频率数据的解释是否合理；3) 指出"能级间距"类比中的内部不一致性

Result: 发现论文在多个方面存在过度解读：CHSH/Bell型计算和Bose-Einstein拟合的解释超出了方法支持范围，"能级间距"类比存在内部不一致性，需要更谨慎地解释关于量子纠缠的结论

Conclusion: 该技术检查旨在建设性地保留有趣的实证观察，同时澄清这些观察在希尔伯特空间意义上的量子纠缠含义，特别是当"能量"由排名定义时，需要更严谨地解释结果

Abstract: This note is a friendly technical check of arXiv:2511.21731v1. I highlight a few places where the manuscript's interpretation of (i) the reported CHSH/Bell-type calculations and (ii) Bose--Einstein (BE) fits to rank-frequency data seems to go beyond what the stated procedures can firmly support. I also point out one internal inconsistency in the "energy-level spacing" analogy. The aim is constructive: to keep the interesting empirical observations, while making clear what they do (and do not) imply about quantum entanglement in the usual Hilbert-space sense, especially when "energy" is defined by rank.

</details>


### [237] [From RLHF to Direct Alignment: A Theoretical Unification of Preference Learning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06108)
*Tarun Raheja,Nilay Pochhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个偏好学习方法的理论统一框架，将现有方法（RLHF、DPO、IPO、KTO、SimPO等）归纳为三个正交轴上的设计选择，为方法选择提供理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型与人类偏好对齐变得日益重要，出现了众多偏好学习方法（RLHF、DPO、IPO、KTO、SimPO等），但缺乏统一的理论框架来指导实践者选择合适的方法。

Method: 提出理论统一框架，将偏好学习方法分解为三个正交轴：偏好模型（目标函数的基础似然模型）、正则化机制（控制与参考策略的偏差）、数据分布（在线vs离线学习及覆盖要求）。通过形式化定义和定理建立关键结果。

Result: 揭示了方法多样性实际上源于三个轴上的设计选择；建立了在线与离线方法的覆盖分离、奖励过度优化的缩放规律、直接对齐方法失败的条件等关键理论结果；识别了失败模式（长度攻击、模式崩溃、似然位移）与特定设计选择的关联。

Conclusion: 该框架将偏好学习从经验性艺术转变为理论基础的学科，为实践者提供了方法选择的决策指南，并通过分析50多篇论文的实证结果验证了理论框架的有效性。

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) with human preferences has become essential for safe and beneficial AI deployment. While Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) established the dominant paradigm, a proliferation of alternatives -- Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), Identity Preference Optimization (IPO), Kahneman-Tversky Optimization (KTO), Simple Preference Optimization (SimPO), and many others -- has left practitioners without clear guidance on method selection. This survey provides a \textit{theoretical unification} of preference learning methods, revealing that the apparent diversity reduces to principled choices along three orthogonal axes: \textbf{(I) Preference Model} (what likelihood model underlies the objective), \textbf{(II) Regularization Mechanism} (how deviation from reference policies is controlled), and \textbf{(III) Data Distribution} (online vs.\ offline learning and coverage requirements). We formalize each axis with precise definitions and theorems, establishing key results including the coverage separation between online and offline methods, scaling laws for reward overoptimization, and conditions under which direct alignment methods fail. Our analysis reveals that failure modes -- length hacking, mode collapse, likelihood displacement -- arise from specific, predictable combinations of design choices. We synthesize empirical findings across 50+ papers and provide a practitioner's decision guide for method selection.
